{'role': 'assistant', 'content': [{'type': 'thinking', 'thinking': "Confirmed: all four edge cases crash. The bug report's case (white pawn on h8) reproduces.\n\nNow the fix. The root cause is in `_moves`: the single-square non-capturing pawn push lacks the `0x88` off-board check. When a pawn sits on its last rank (white on rank 8, black on rank 1), the push goes off-board and creates a move to an invalid square, which later crashes `_pieceKey` with a BigInt type error.\n\nMinimal fix: add `0x88` check to the single push, matching the capture-loop style:\n\n```ts\n// single square, non-capturing\nto = from + PAWN_OFFSETS[us][0]\nif (!(to & 0x88) && !this._board[to]) {\n  addMove(moves, us, from, to, PAWN)\n\n  // double square\n  to = from + PAWN_OFFSETS[us][1]\n  if (SECOND_RANK[us] === rank(from) && !this._board[to]) {\n    addMove(moves, us, from, to, PAWN, undefined, BITS.BIG_PAWN)\n  }\n}\n```\n\nShould I also guard the double push? A pawn on SECOND_RANK has single push always on-board (rank 2 → rank 3/4, all within board). So the double push is never off-board when it can actually trigger. But for defensive consistency, the double push check `!this._board[to]` with `to` off-board would also be a problem if it could trigger... it can't, since it only runs if `SECOND_RANK[us] === rank(from)`, and rank(from) is only rank 2 for white / rank 7 for black, where both single and double pushes stay on the board.\n\nActually wait, I should double check the double push: white on rank 2 (indices 96-103). to = from - 32 = indices 64-71 → rank 4.
